Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on protein structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 185. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 328 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to protein theory, we decompose the problem into 185 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 328. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
